A: ["Astrosat"]
Q: Gaia is a space observatory of the European Space Agency, what is Astrosat?
A: "Astrosat is India's first dedicated multi-wavelength space observatory. It was launched on a PSLV-XL on 28 September 2015. Unlike Gaia, which primarily focuses on astrometry and mapping the positions, proper motions, and radial velocities of stars, Astrosat is designed to study celestial objects across different parts of the electromagnetic spectrum, including ultraviolet, optical, and low-energy X-ray regions."
A: Astrosat is India's first dedicated multi-wavelength space observatory. It was launched on a PSLV-XL on 28 September 2015.
